Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The speech operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 328. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 396 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to speech theory, we decompose the problem into 328 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 396. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
